function AdminPage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const { isAdmin, checking } = useIsAdmin(user?.id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (loading || checking) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Checking your access…
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-20 text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Administrator sign-in required</h1>
          <Button className="mt-6" onClick={() => navigate({ to: "/auth" })}>
            Sign in
          </Button>
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  if (!isAdmin) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-20 text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">You don't have admin access</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Ask an existing administrator to grant your account the admin role.
          </p>
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Admin dashboard</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Manage villages, facilities and incoming reports.
        </p>

        <Tabs defaultValue="overview" className="mt-6">
          <TabsList>
            <TabsTrigger value="overview">Overview</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="villages">Villages</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="facilities">Facilities</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="reports">Reports</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
          <TabsContent value="overview">
            <OverviewTab />
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="villages">
            <VillagesTab />
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="facilities">
            <FacilitiesTab />
          </TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="reports">
            <ReportsTab />
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

function OverviewTab() {
  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets", "all"], queryFn: getAllAssets });
  const stats = summarise(assets);
  const byCategory = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<AssetCategory, number>();
    assets.forEach((a) => map.set(a.category, (map.get(a.category) ?? 0) + 1));
    return [...map.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  }, [assets]);
  const max = byCategory[0]?.[1] ?? 1;

  return (
    <div className="mt-6 space-y-8">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-5">
        {[
          ["Villages", villages.length],
          ["Facilities", stats.total],
          ["Available", stats.available],
          ["Maintenance", stats.maintenance],
          ["Unavailable", stats.unavailable],
        ].map(([label, value]) => (
          <div key={String(label)} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
            <p className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-primary">{value}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{label}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <section>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Facilities by category</h2>
        {byCategory.length ? (
          <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
            {byCategory.map(([cat, count]) => (
              <li key={cat} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="w-40 shrink-0 text-sm font-ui">
                  {CATEGORY_META[cat].icon} {CATEGORY_META[cat].label}
                </span>
                <span className="h-3 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted">
                  <span
                    className="block h-full rounded-full bg-leaf transition-all duration-700"
                    style={{ width: `${(count / max) * 100}%` }}
                  />
                </span>
                <span className="w-8 text-right text-sm font-ui">{count}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            No facilities recorded yet.
          </p>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function VillagesTab() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    district: "",
    state: "",
    latitude: "",
    longitude: "",
    description: "",
  });
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function locate() {
    if (!form.name.trim()) return;
    try {
      const res = await geocodeVillage({
        data: { query: [form.name, form.district, form.state].filter(Boolean).join(", ") },
      });
      const first = res.results[0];
      if (!first) {
        toast.error("Google Maps did not find that place.");
        return;
      }
      setForm((f) => ({
        ...f,
        district: f.district || (first.district ?? ""),
        state: f.state || (first.state ?? ""),
        latitude: String(first.latitude),
        longitude: String(first.longitude),
      }));
      toast.success("Coordinates filled from Google Maps.");
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error((e as Error).message);
    }
  }

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = await supabase.from("villages").insert({
      name: form.name.trim(),
      district: form.district || null,
      state: form.state || null,
      latitude: form.latitude ? Number(form.latitude) : null,
      longitude: form.longitude ? Number(form.longitude) : null,
      description: form.description || null,
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(error.message);
      return;
    }
    toast.success("Village added.");
    setForm({ name: "", district: "", state: "", latitude: "", longitude: "", description: "" });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["villages"] });
  }

  async function remove(v: VillageRow) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete ${v.name} and all its facilities?`)) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.from("villages").delete().eq("id", v.id);
    if (error) toast.error(error.message);
    else {
      toast.success("Village deleted.");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["villages"] });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-6 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[380px_1fr]">
      <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border bg-card p-5 shadow-soft">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Add a village</h2>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Village name</Label>
          <Input
            required
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>District</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.district}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, district: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>State</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.state}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, state: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Latitude</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.latitude}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, latitude: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Longitude</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.longitude}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, longitude: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button type="button" variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={locate}>
          Locate on Google Maps
        </Button>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Description</Label>
          <Textarea
            rows={3}
            value={form.description}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>
          {busy ? "Saving…" : "Add village"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Registered villages</h2>
        {villages.length ? (
          <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
            {villages.map((v) => (
              <li
                key={v.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3 shadow-soft"
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                    {villageLabel(v)}
                    {v.latitude != null ? ` · ${Number(v.latitude).toFixed(4)}, ${Number(v.longitude).toFixed(4)}` : " · no coordinates"}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => void remove(v)}>
                  Delete
                </Button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">No villages added yet.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FacilitiesTab() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets", "all"], queryFn: getAllAssets });
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    village_id: "",
    name: "",
    category: "other" as AssetCategory,
    description: "",
    latitude: "",
    longitude: "",
    status: "unverified" as AssetStatus,
    condition: "",
    contact: "",
    operating_hours: "",
    verified: false,
  });

  const village = villages.find((v) => v.id === form.village_id);
  const center =
    form.latitude && form.longitude
      ? { lat: Number(form.latitude), lng: Number(form.longitude) }
      : village?.latitude != null
        ? { lat: Number(village.latitude), lng: Number(village.longitude) }
        : null;

  async function uploadPhoto(): Promise<string | null> {
    if (!photo) return null;
    const path = `${crypto.randomUUID()}-${photo.name.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, "_")}`;
    const { error } = await supabase.storage.from("facility-photos").upload(path, photo);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(`Photo upload failed: ${error.message}`);
      return null;
    }
    const { data } = await supabase.storage
      .from("facility-photos")
      .createSignedUrl(path, 60 * 60 * 24 * 365);
    return data?.signedUrl ?? null;
  }

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!form.village_id) {
      toast.error("Choose a village first.");
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    const photo_url = await uploadPhoto();
    const { error } = await supabase.from("assets").insert({
      village_id: form.village_id,
      name: form.name.trim(),
      category: form.category,
      description: form.description || null,
      latitude: form.latitude ? Number(form.latitude) : null,
      longitude: form.longitude ? Number(form.longitude) : null,
      status: form.status,
      condition: form.condition || null,
      contact: form.contact || null,
      operating_hours: form.operating_hours || null,
      verified: form.verified,
      photo_url,
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(error.message);
      return;
    }
    toast.success("Facility added.");
    setForm({ ...form, name: "", description: "", latitude: "", longitude: "", condition: "" });
    setPhoto(null);
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] });
  }

  async function update(asset: AssetRow, patch: Partial<AssetRow>) {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("assets").update(patch).eq("id", asset.id);
    if (error) toast.error(error.message);
    else void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] });
  }

  async function remove(asset: AssetRow) {
    if (!confirm(`Delete ${asset.name}?`)) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.from("assets").delete().eq("id", asset.id);
    if (error) toast.error(error.message);
    else {
      toast.success("Facility deleted.");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] });
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-6 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[420px_1fr]">
      <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border bg-card p-5 shadow-soft">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Add a facility</h2>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Village</Label>
          <select
            value={form.village_id}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, village_id: e.target.value })}
            className="h-10 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
          >
            <option value="">Select a village…</option>
            {villages.map((v) => (
              <option key={v.id} value={v.id}>
                {villageLabel(v)}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Facility name</Label>
          <Input
            required
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Category</Label>
            <select
              value={form.category}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value as AssetCategory })}
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
            >
              {CATEGORY_LIST.map((c) => (
                <option key={c} value={c}>
                  {CATEGORY_META[c].label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Status</Label>
            <select
              value={form.status}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, status: e.target.value as AssetStatus })}
              className="h-10 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
            >
              {STATUS_LIST.map((s) => (
                <option key={s} value={s}>
                  {STATUS_META[s].label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Description</Label>
          <Textarea
            rows={3}
            value={form.description}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Latitude</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.latitude}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, latitude: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Longitude</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.longitude}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, longitude: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Click anywhere on the map to set the facility location.
        </p>
        <FacilityMap
          className="h-56"
          center={center}
          zoom={15}
          markers={
            form.latitude && form.longitude
              ? [
                  {
                    id: "new",
                    name: form.name || "New facility",
                    latitude: Number(form.latitude),
                    longitude: Number(form.longitude),
                    status: form.status,
                    category: form.category,
                  },
                ]
              : []
          }
          onMapClick={(c) =>
            setForm((f) => ({ ...f, latitude: String(c.lat), longitude: String(c.lng) }))
          }
        />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Condition</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.condition}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, condition: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Operating hours</Label>
            <Input
              value={form.operating_hours}
              onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, operating_hours: e.target.value })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Contact</Label>
          <Input
            value={form.contact}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, contact: e.target.value })}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Photo</Label>
          <Input
            type="file"
            accept="image/*"
            onChange={(e) => setPhoto(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)}
          />
        </div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-ui">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={form.verified}
            onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, verified: e.target.checked })}
          />
          Mark as verified
        </label>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={busy}>
          {busy ? "Saving…" : "Add facility"}
        </Button>
      </form>

      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Recorded facilities</h2>
        {assets.length ? (
          <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
            {assets.map((a) => (
              <li key={a.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-3 shadow-soft">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                      {CATEGORY_META[a.category].icon} {a.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                      {villages.find((v) => v.id === a.village_id)?.name ?? "Unknown village"}
                      {a.verified ? " · verified" : " · not verified"}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <select
                      value={a.status}
                      onChange={(e) => void update(a, { status: e.target.value as AssetStatus })}
                      className="h-9 rounded-md border bg-background px-2 text-xs font-ui"
                    >
                      {STATUS_LIST.map((s) => (
                        <option key={s} value={s}>
                          {STATUS_META[s].label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      variant="secondary"
                      onClick={() => void update(a, { verified: !a.verified })}
                    >
                      {a.verified ? "Unverify" : "Verify"}
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => void remove(a)}>
                      Delete
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">No facilities recorded yet.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ReportsTab() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { data: reports = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin-reports"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("reports")
        .select("*")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data ?? [];
    },
  });

  async function setStatus(id: string, status: string) {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from("reports")
      .update({ status: status as never })
      .eq("id", id);
    if (error) toast.error(error.message);
    else void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin-reports"] });
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-6">
      {reports.length ? (
        <ul className="space-y-3">
          {reports.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium capitalize">
                  {r.report_type.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                </p>
                <select
                  value={r.status}
                  onChange={(e) => void setStatus(r.id, e.target.value)}
                  className="h-9 rounded-md border bg-background px-2 text-xs font-ui"
                >
                  {["open", "in_review", "resolved", "rejected"].map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>
                      {s.replace("_", " ")}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{r.description}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">No reports submitted yet.</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/routes/ask.tsx

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { FacilityCard } from "@/components/FacilityCard";
import FacilityMap, { type MapMarkerPoint } from "@/components/FacilityMap";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { parseQuery, type ParsedQuery } from "@/lib/nlp";
import { CATEGORY_META, type AssetCategory } from "@/lib/categories";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import {
  discoverVillageFacilities,
  geocodeVillage,
  type DiscoveredFacility,
  type GeocodedPlace,
} from "@/lib/maps.functions";
import {
  getAllVillages,
  getVillageAssets,
  summarise,
  villageLabel,
  type VillageRow,
} from "@/lib/villages";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/ask")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "Ask about a village — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content:
          "Ask a question in plain language about any village's facilities and get answers from verified records.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Ask about a village — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content:
          "Ask a question in plain language about any village's facilities and get answers from verified records.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: AskPage,
});

const EXAMPLES = [
  "Is there a hospital?",
  "Find schools",
  "Which facilities need maintenance?",
  "How many facilities are available?",
  "Where is the water supply?",
];

interface ResolvedVillage {
  registered: VillageRow | null;
  external: GeocodedPlace | null;
  notFound: boolean;
}

export default function AskPage() {
  const [villageName, setVillageName] = useState("");
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState("");
  const [parsed, setParsed] = useState<ParsedQuery | null>(null);
  const [resolved, setResolved] = useState<ResolvedVillage | null>(null);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [searchError, setSearchError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [discovered, setDiscovered] = useState<DiscoveredFacility[] | null>(null);
  const [discoverError, setDiscoverError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [discovering, setDiscovering] = useState(false);

  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const village = resolved?.registered ?? null;

  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["assets", village?.id],
    queryFn: () => getVillageAssets(village!.id),
    enabled: Boolean(village),
  });

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    if (!parsed) return [];
    let list = assets;
    if (parsed.categories.length) {
      list = list.filter((a) => parsed.categories.includes(a.category));
    }
    if (parsed.intent === "maintenance") list = list.filter((a) => a.status === "maintenance");
    if (parsed.intent === "unavailable") list = list.filter((a) => a.status === "unavailable");
    if (parsed.terms.length && !parsed.categories.length) {
      list = list.filter((a) =>
        parsed.terms.some(
          (t) =>
            a.name.toLowerCase().includes(t) ||
            (a.description ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(t) ||
            CATEGORY_META[a.category].label.toLowerCase().includes(t),
        ),
      );
    }
    return list;
  }, [parsed, assets]);

  const stats = summarise(assets);
  const displayName = village?.name ?? resolved?.external?.name ?? undefined;
  const answer = parsed && resolved ? buildAnswer(parsed, results.length, stats, displayName) : null;

  // Map always shows every facility of the resolved village; the question
  // only filters the answer text and the highlighted result cards.
  const markers: MapMarkerPoint[] = [
    ...assets
      .filter((a) => a.latitude != null && a.longitude != null)
      .map((a) => ({
        id: a.id,
        name: a.name,
        subtitle: CATEGORY_META[a.category].label,
        latitude: Number(a.latitude),
        longitude: Number(a.longitude),
        category: a.category,
        status: a.status,
      })),
    ...(resolved?.external
      ? [
          {
            id: `external-${resolved.external.placeId}`,
            name: resolved.external.name,
            subtitle: resolved.external.formattedAddress,
            latitude: resolved.external.latitude,
            longitude: resolved.external.longitude,
            external: true,
          },
        ]
      : []),
    ...(discovered ?? []).map((f) => ({
      id: `discovered-${f.id}`,
      name: f.name,
      subtitle: f.address,
      latitude: f.latitude,
      longitude: f.longitude,
      category: f.category as AssetCategory,
      status: "unverified" as const,
    })),
  ];

  // When the user asked about a specific facility type, highlight matching
  // Google Maps results first; otherwise show everything found nearby.
  const discoveredShown = useMemo(() => {
    const list = discovered ?? [];
    if (!parsed?.categories.length) return list;
    const matching = list.filter((f) => parsed.categories.includes(f.category as AssetCategory));
    return matching.length ? matching : list;
  }, [discovered, parsed]);

  const center =
    village?.latitude != null && village?.longitude != null
      ? { lat: Number(village.latitude), lng: Number(village.longitude) }
      : resolved?.external
        ? { lat: resolved.external.latitude, lng: resolved.external.longitude }
        : null;

  // Discover facilities around the resolved place via Google Maps — works for
  // any village or city, registered or not. Signed-in only (billable calls).
  useEffect(() => {
    setDiscovered(null);
    setDiscoverError(null);
    if (!center || !user || !resolved || resolved.notFound) return;
    let active = true;
    setDiscovering(true);
    discoverVillageFacilities({ data: { latitude: center.lat, longitude: center.lng } })
      .then((r) => active && setDiscovered(r.facilities))
      .catch((e) => active && setDiscoverError((e as Error).message))
      .finally(() => active && setDiscovering(false));
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [center?.lat, center?.lng, user, resolved]);

  async function ask() {
    const q = question.trim();
    const name = villageName.trim();
    if (!name) return;
    setSearching(true);
    setSearchError(null);
    setParsed(q ? parseQuery(q) : null);
    try {
      const needle = name.toLowerCase();
      const match =
        villages.find((v) => v.name.toLowerCase() === needle) ??
        villages.find((v) => v.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) ??
        null;
      if (match) {
        setResolved({ registered: match, external: null, notFound: false });
      } else {
        const { results: places } = await geocodeVillage({ data: { query: name } });
        const first = places[0] ?? null;
        setResolved({ registered: null, external: first, notFound: !first });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setResolved(null);
      setSearchError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Village search failed.");
    } finally {
      setSearching(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-5xl px-4 py-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Ask about a village</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Type any village name, then ask in your own words. Answers come from recorded facility
          data; the map locates the village through Google Maps.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[260px_1fr_auto]">
          <Input
            value={villageName}
            onChange={(e) => setVillageName(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter") void ask();
            }}
            placeholder="Village name..."
            className="h-11"
            list="ask-village-suggestions"
          />
          <datalist id="ask-village-suggestions">
            {villages.map((v) => (
              <option key={v.id} value={v.name}>
                {villageLabel(v)}
              </option>
            ))}
          </datalist>
          <Input
            value={question}
            onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter") void ask();
            }}
            placeholder="Ask about a facility... (optional)"
            className="h-11"
          />
          <Button
            className="h-11"
            disabled={!villageName.trim() || searching}
            onClick={() => void ask()}
          >
            {searching ? "Searching…" : "Ask"}
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {EXAMPLES.map((ex) => (
            <button
              key={ex}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setQuestion(ex)}
              className="rounded-full border bg-card px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent/30 font-ui"
            >
              {ex}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {searchError ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 p-4 text-sm font-ui">
            {searchError}
          </p>
        ) : null}

        {resolved?.notFound ? (
          <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Google Maps could not find a village with that name. Check the spelling and try again.
          </p>
        ) : null}

        {answer ? (
          <div className="mt-8 animate-fade-up rounded-2xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-soft">
            <p className="text-lg font-medium">{answer}</p>
            {parsed?.intent === "count" && village ? (
              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
                <MiniStat label="Available" value={stats.available} />
                <MiniStat label="Maintenance" value={stats.maintenance} />
                <MiniStat label="Unavailable" value={stats.unavailable} />
                <MiniStat label="Not verified" value={stats.unverified} />
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {resolved?.external && !village ? (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-5 font-ui">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {resolved.external.name} was located on the map. It is not yet in the GramMap records,
              so the facilities below come from Google Maps and are not verified. A missing record
              does not mean a facility does not exist.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-3">
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" asChild>
                <Link to="/report">Report a missing facility</Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {resolved && (markers.length || center) ? (
          <div className="mt-6">
            <FacilityMap markers={markers} center={center} className="h-[320px]" />
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {parsed && results.length ? (
          <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {results.map((a) => (
              <FacilityCard key={a.id} asset={a} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {resolved && !resolved.notFound && center ? (
          <section className="mt-8">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">
              Facilities near {displayName ?? "this place"} on Google Maps
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
              Within 5 km. From Google Maps — not yet verified by GramMap, so status and condition
              are not known.
            </p>
            {!user ? (
              <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm font-ui">
                <Link to="/auth" className="font-medium text-primary underline">
                  Sign in
                </Link>{" "}
                to see the facilities Google Maps lists around this place.
              </div>
            ) : discovering ? (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">Finding facilities…</p>
            ) : discoverError ? (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-destructive font-ui">{discoverError}</p>
            ) : discovered && discoveredShown.length ? (
              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {discoveredShown.map((f) => {
                  const meta = CATEGORY_META[f.category as AssetCategory] ?? CATEGORY_META.other;
                  return (
                    <div key={f.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                        {meta.icon} {meta.label} · ⚪ Not verified
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-medium">{f.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                        {f.address || "Information not available"}
                      </p>
                      <a
                        className="mt-2 inline-block text-sm text-primary underline font-ui"
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        href={`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${f.latitude},${f.longitude}&destination_place_id=${f.id}`}
                      >
                        Get directions
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : discovered ? (
              <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                Google Maps lists no facilities within 5 km. A missing record does not mean the
                facility does not exist.
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </section>
        ) : null}

        {village ? (
          <section className="mt-8">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">All facilities in {village.name}</h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                  {stats.total} recorded · {stats.available} available · {stats.maintenance} under
                  maintenance · {stats.unavailable} unavailable · {stats.unverified} not verified
                </p>
              </div>
              <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
                <Link to="/village/$villageId" params={{ villageId: village.id }}>
                  Open village dashboard
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
            {assets.length ? (
              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {assets.map((a) => (
                  <FacilityCard key={a.id} asset={a} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                Facility information is currently unavailable for this village. A missing record
                does not mean the facility does not exist.
              </p>
            )}
          </section>
        ) : null}

        {parsed && village && !results.length ? (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-5 font-ui">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Facility information is currently unavailable. A missing record does not mean the
              facility does not exist.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" asChild>
                <Link to="/village/$villageId" params={{ villageId: village.id }}>
                  Search nearby
                </Link>
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" asChild>
                <Link to="/report" search={{ villageId: village.id }}>
                  Report a missing facility
                </Link>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

function MiniStat({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border bg-background p-3 text-center">
      <p className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-primary">{value}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function buildAnswer(
  parsed: ParsedQuery,
  count: number,
  stats: ReturnType<typeof summarise>,
  villageName?: string,
) {
  const where = villageName ? `in ${villageName}` : "in this village";
  const subject = parsed.categories.length
    ? parsed.categories.map((c) => CATEGORY_META[c].label.toLowerCase()).join(" and ")
    : "matching facilities";

  switch (parsed.intent) {
    case "count":
      return `${stats.total} facilities are recorded ${where}: ${stats.available} available, ${stats.maintenance} needing maintenance, ${stats.unavailable} unavailable and ${stats.unverified} not verified.`;
    case "maintenance":
      return count
        ? `${count} ${subject} ${count === 1 ? "needs" : "need"} maintenance ${where}.`
        : `No facility ${where} is currently recorded as needing maintenance.`;
    case "unavailable":
      return count
        ? `${count} ${subject} ${count === 1 ? "is" : "are"} recorded as unavailable ${where}.`
        : `No facility ${where} is currently recorded as unavailable.`;
    case "location":
      return count
        ? `Found ${count} ${subject} ${where} — see the map markers below.`
        : `No location is recorded for ${subject} ${where}.`;
    case "nearby":
    case "directions":
      return count
        ? `Found ${count} ${subject} ${where}. Use "Get directions" on any result.`
        : `Nothing matching is recorded ${where}. Try the nearby search on the village page.`;
    default:
      return count
        ? `Yes — ${count} ${subject} ${count === 1 ? "is" : "are"} recorded ${where}.`
        : `No verified record of ${subject} ${where} yet.`;
  }
}

FILE: src/routes/auth.tsx
================================================================================

import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { lovable } from "@/integrations/lovable/index";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/auth")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "Sign in — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content: "Sign in to report facility issues and search nearby places in GramMap.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Sign in — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content: "Sign in to report facility issues and search nearby places in GramMap.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: AuthPage,
});

function AuthPage() {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"signin" | "signup">("signin");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) navigate({ to: "/", replace: true });
  }, [user, navigate]);

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setNotice(null);
    try {
      if (mode === "signup") {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
          email,
          password,
          options: {
            emailRedirectTo: window.location.origin,
            data: { name },
          },
        });
        if (error) throw error;
        if (!data.session) {
          setNotice("Check your email to confirm your account, then sign in.");
        }
      } else {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
        if (error) throw error;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error((err as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleGoogle() {
    const result = await lovable.auth.signInWithOAuth("google", {
      redirect_uri: window.location.origin,
    });
    if (result.error) {
      toast.error("Google sign-in did not complete.");
      return;
    }
    if (result.redirected) return;
    navigate({ to: "/" });
  }

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-md px-4 py-16">
        <div className="animate-fade-up rounded-2xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-lift">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">
            {mode === "signin" ? "Sign in to GramMap" : "Create your GramMap account"}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Signing in lets you report facility issues and search nearby places.
          </p>

          <form className="mt-6 space-y-4" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            {mode === "signup" ? (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="name">Name</Label>
                <Input id="name" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} required />
              </div>
            ) : null}
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
              <Input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                required
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
              <Input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                value={password}
                minLength={6}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                required
              />
            </div>
            <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>
              {busy ? "Please wait…" : mode === "signin" ? "Sign in" : "Create account"}
            </Button>
          </form>

          {notice ? (
            <p className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-accent/30 p-3 text-sm font-ui">{notice}</p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="my-5 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-border" /> or <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-border" />
          </div>

          <Button variant="outline" className="w-full" onClick={handleGoogle}>
            Continue with Google
          </Button>

          <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            {mode === "signin" ? "New to GramMap?" : "Already have an account?"}{" "}
            <button
              type="button"
              className="text-primary underline"
              onClick={() => setMode(mode === "signin" ? "signup" : "signin")}
            >
              {mode === "signin" ? "Create an account" : "Sign in"}
            </button>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/routes/explore.tsx
================================================================================

import { createFileRoute, Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { z } from "zod";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import FacilityMap, { type MapMarkerPoint } from "@/components/FacilityMap";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { getAllAssets, getAllVillages, summarise, villageLabel, type AssetRow } from "@/lib/villages";
import { FacilityCard } from "@/components/FacilityCard";
import { geocodeVillage, discoverVillageFacilities, type GeocodedPlace, type DiscoveredFacility } from "@/lib/maps.functions";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { CATEGORY_META, type AssetCategory } from "@/lib/categories";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/explore")({
  validateSearch: z.object({ q: z.string().optional() }),
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "Explore the village map — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content: "Browse mapped villages and their recorded facilities on an interactive map.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Explore the village map — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content: "Browse mapped villages and their recorded facilities on an interactive map.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: ExplorePage,
});

function ExplorePage() {
  const { q } = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [term, setTerm] = useState(q ?? "");
  const [lookup, setLookup] = useState<GeocodedPlace[] | null>(null);
  const [lookupError, setLookupError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [discovered, setDiscovered] = useState<DiscoveredFacility[] | null>(null);
  const [discoverError, setDiscoverError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [discovering, setDiscovering] = useState(false);

  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets", "all"], queryFn: getAllAssets });

  const registered = useMemo(
    () =>
      villages.filter((v) =>
        term.trim() ? v.name.toLowerCase().includes(term.trim().toLowerCase()) : true,
      ),
    [villages, term],
  );

  // Facilities that belong to the village(s) matching the search term.
  const matchedAssets: AssetRow[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!term.trim()) return [];
    const ids = new Set(registered.map((v) => v.id));
    return assets.filter((a) => ids.has(a.village_id));
  }, [assets, registered, term]);

  const matchedSummary = useMemo(() => summarise(matchedAssets), [matchedAssets]);

  async function runLookup(value: string) {
    const query = value.trim();
    if (query.length < 2) return;
    setSearching(true);
    setLookupError(null);
    try {
      const res = await geocodeVillage({ data: { query } });
      setLookup(res.results);
      setDiscovered(null);
      setDiscoverError(null);
    } catch (e) {
      setLookupError((e as Error).message);
      setLookup([]);
    } finally {
      setSearching(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (q && q.trim().length >= 2) {
      setTerm(q);
      void runLookup(q);
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [q]);

  const firstPlace = lookup?.[0];
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!firstPlace || !user) return;
    let active = true;
    setDiscovering(true);
    discoverVillageFacilities({ data: { latitude: firstPlace.latitude, longitude: firstPlace.longitude } })
      .then((r) => active && setDiscovered(r.facilities))
      .catch((e) => active && setDiscoverError((e as Error).message))
      .finally(() => active && setDiscovering(false));
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [firstPlace, user]);

  const markers: MapMarkerPoint[] = useMemo(() => {
    // Always show every registered village on the map.
    const villageMarkers = villages
      .filter((v) => v.latitude != null && v.longitude != null)
      .map((v) => ({
        id: `village-${v.id}`,
        name: v.name,
        subtitle: villageLabel(v),
        latitude: Number(v.latitude),
        longitude: Number(v.longitude),
      }));
    // Facility markers: after a search, only the matched village's facilities;
    // otherwise all recorded facilities.
    const facilitySource = term.trim() ? matchedAssets : assets;
    const assetMarkers = facilitySource
      .filter((a) => a.latitude != null && a.longitude != null)
      .map((a) => ({
        id: a.id,
        name: a.name,
        subtitle: "Recorded facility",
        latitude: Number(a.latitude),
        longitude: Number(a.longitude),
        category: a.category,
        status: a.status,
      }));
    const found = (lookup ?? [])
      .slice(0, 5)
      .map((p) => ({
        id: p.placeId,
        name: p.name,
        subtitle: p.formattedAddress,
        latitude: p.latitude,
        longitude: p.longitude,
        external: true,
      }));
    const disc = (discovered ?? []).map((f) => ({
      id: `g-${f.id}`,
      name: f.name,
      subtitle: f.address,
      latitude: f.latitude,
      longitude: f.longitude,
      category: f.category as AssetCategory,
      external: true,
    }));
    return [...villageMarkers, ...assetMarkers, ...found, ...disc];
  }, [villages, assets, matchedAssets, term, lookup, discovered]);

  const first = lookup?.[0];
  const firstMatched = registered.find((v) => v.latitude != null && v.longitude != null);
  const center = first
    ? { lat: first.latitude, lng: first.longitude }
    : term.trim() && firstMatched
      ? { lat: Number(firstMatched.latitude), lng: Number(firstMatched.longitude) }
      : null;

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Explore the map</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Search any village by name. Google Maps locates it; recorded facilities come from the
          GramMap database.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <Input
            value={term}
            onChange={(e) => setTerm(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter") {
                navigate({ to: "/explore", search: { q: term.trim() || undefined } });
                void runLookup(term);
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Search for a village..."
            className="h-12"
          />
          <Button
            className="h-12 px-8"
            onClick={() => {
              navigate({ to: "/explore", search: { q: term.trim() || undefined } });
              void runLookup(term);
            }}
            disabled={searching}
          >
            {searching ? "Locating…" : "Locate village"}
          </Button>
        </div>

        {lookupError ? (
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-destructive font-ui">{lookupError}</p>
        ) : null}

        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]">
          <FacilityMap markers={markers} center={center} className="h-[420px] lg:h-[560px]" />

          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                Inside GramMap
              </h2>
              {registered.length ? (
                <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                  {registered.slice(0, 12).map((v) => (
                    <li key={v.id}>
                      <Link
                        to="/village/$villageId"
                        params={{ villageId: v.id }}
                        className="block rounded-xl border bg-card p-3 shadow-soft transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-lift"
                      >
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium">{v.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{villageLabel(v)}</p>
                      </Link>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                  No village in the database matches this search yet.
                </p>
              )}
            </section>

            {lookup ? (
              <section>
                <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                  Found on Google Maps
                </h2>
                {lookup.length ? (
                  <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                    {lookup.slice(0, 5).map((p) => (
                      <li
                        key={p.placeId}
                        className="rounded-xl border border-sky/40 bg-sky/10 p-3 shadow-soft"
                      >
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium">{p.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{p.formattedAddress}</p>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                ) : (
                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                    Google Maps did not return a location for that name.
                  </p>
                )}
              </section>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>

        {term.trim() && registered.length ? (
          <section className="mt-8">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">
                  Facilities in {registered.map((v) => v.name).slice(0, 3).join(", ")}
                  {registered.length > 3 ? ` and ${registered.length - 3} more` : ""}
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                  {matchedSummary.total} recorded · {matchedSummary.available} available ·{" "}
                  {matchedSummary.maintenance} under maintenance · {matchedSummary.unavailable}{" "}
                  unavailable · {matchedSummary.unverified} not verified
                </p>
              </div>
              {registered.length === 1 && registered[0] ? (
                <Button asChild variant="outline">
                  <Link to="/village/$villageId" params={{ villageId: registered[0].id }}>
                    Open village dashboard
                  </Link>
                </Button>
              ) : null}
            </div>

            {matchedAssets.length ? (
              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {matchedAssets.map((a) => (
                  <FacilityCard key={a.id} asset={a} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                Facility information is currently unavailable for this village. A missing record
                does not mean the facility does not exist.
              </p>
            )}
          </section>
        ) : null}
        {firstPlace ? (
          <section className="mt-8">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Facilities near {firstPlace.name} on Google Maps</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
              Within 5 km of the village. From Google Maps — not yet verified by GramMap, so status and condition are not known.
            </p>
            {!user ? (
              <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm font-ui">
                <Link to="/auth" className="font-medium text-primary underline">Sign in</Link> to see the facilities Google Maps lists around this village.
              </div>
            ) : discovering ? (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">Finding facilities…</p>
            ) : discoverError ? (
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-destructive font-ui">{discoverError}</p>
            ) : discovered && discovered.length ? (
              <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {discovered.map((f) => {
                  const meta = CATEGORY_META[f.category as AssetCategory] ?? CATEGORY_META.other;
                  return (
                    <div key={f.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{meta.icon} {meta.label} · ⚪ Not verified</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-medium">{f.name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{f.address || "Information not available"}</p>
                      <a
                        className="mt-2 inline-block text-sm text-primary underline font-ui"
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        href={`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${f.latitude},${f.longitude}&destination_place_id=${f.id}`}
                      >Get directions</a>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            ) : discovered ? (
              <p className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                Google Maps lists no facilities within 5 km. A missing record does not mean the facility does not exist.
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </section>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/routes/facilities.tsx
================================================================================

import { createFileRoute, Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { z } from "zod";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { FacilityCard } from "@/components/FacilityCard";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  CATEGORY_LIST,
  CATEGORY_META,
  STATUS_LIST,
  STATUS_META,
  type AssetCategory,
  type AssetStatus,
} from "@/lib/categories";
import { getAllAssets, getAllVillages, villageLabel } from "@/lib/villages";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/facilities")({
  validateSearch: z.object({
    category: z.string().optional(),
    status: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "All recorded facilities — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content:
          "Browse every facility recorded in GramMap by category, status and village.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "All recorded facilities — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content: "Browse every facility recorded in GramMap by category, status and village.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: FacilitiesPage,
});

function FacilitiesPage() {
  const search = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [term, setTerm] = useState("");

  const { data: assets = [], isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["assets", "all"],
    queryFn: getAllAssets,
  });
  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });

  const villageById = useMemo(
    () => new Map(villages.map((v) => [v.id, v])),
    [villages],
  );

  const category = (search.category ?? "all") as AssetCategory | "all";
  const status = (search.status ?? "all") as AssetStatus | "all";

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () =>
      assets.filter((a) => {
        if (category !== "all" && a.category !== category) return false;
        if (status !== "all" && a.status !== status) return false;
        if (term.trim()) {
          const t = term.trim().toLowerCase();
          const v = villageById.get(a.village_id);
          return (
            a.name.toLowerCase().includes(t) ||
            (a.description ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(t) ||
            (v?.name ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(t)
          );
        }
        return true;
      }),
    [assets, category, status, term, villageById],
  );

  function setFilter(next: { category?: string | undefined; status?: string | undefined }) {
    navigate({ to: "/facilities", search: { ...search, ...next } });
  }

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Facilities</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Every facility recorded in GramMap, across all mapped villages.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_200px_200px]">
          <Input
            value={term}
            onChange={(e) => setTerm(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search facilities or villages..."
            className="h-11"
          />
          <select
            value={category}
            onChange={(e) => setFilter({ category: e.target.value === "all" ? undefined : e.target.value })}
            className="h-11 rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
          >
            <option value="all">All categories</option>
            {CATEGORY_LIST.map((c) => (
              <option key={c} value={c}>
                {CATEGORY_META[c].label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={status}
            onChange={(e) => setFilter({ status: e.target.value === "all" ? undefined : e.target.value })}
            className="h-11 rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
          >
            <option value="all">All statuses</option>
            {STATUS_LIST.map((s) => (
              <option key={s} value={s}>
                {STATUS_META[s].label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="mt-10 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">Loading facilities…</p>
        ) : filtered.length ? (
          <div className="mt-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {filtered.map((a) => {
              const v = villageById.get(a.village_id);
              return (
                <div key={a.id} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {v ? (
                    <Link
                      to="/village/$villageId"
                      params={{ villageId: v.id }}
                      className="text-xs text-primary underline font-ui"
                    >
                      {villageLabel(v)}
                    </Link>
                  ) : null}
                  <FacilityCard asset={a} />
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="mt-10 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Facility information is currently unavailable for these filters.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

FILE: src/routes/index.tsx
