import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { VillageSearch } from "@/components/VillageSearch";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { CATEGORY_LIST, CATEGORY_META, STATUS_META, STATUS_LIST } from "@/lib/categories";
import { getAllAssets, getAllVillages, summarise } from "@/lib/villages";
import heroImage from "@/assets/village-hero.jpg";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "GramMap — Discover your village facilities" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content:
          "Search any village and explore its facilities, resources and infrastructure through an interactive map.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "GramMap — Discover your village facilities" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content:
          "Search any village and explore its facilities, resources and infrastructure through an interactive map.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: HomePage,
});

function HomePage() {
  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["assets", "all"], queryFn: getAllAssets });
  const stats = summarise(assets);

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <section className="relative overflow-hidden">
        <img
          src={heroImage}
          alt="Village landscape with fields, houses, a well and a water tank"
          width={1920}
          height={1088}
          className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
        />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-hero-gradient" />
        <div className="relative mx-auto flex w-full max-w-4xl flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center md:py-32">
          <span className="animate-fade-in rounded-full border border-primary-foreground/30 bg-background/20 px-4 py-1 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-primary-foreground backdrop-blur font-ui">
            Ask about your village. Find its facilities on the map.
          </span>
          <h1 className="mt-6 animate-fade-up text-4xl font-bold leading-tight text-primary-foreground drop-shadow-sm md:text-6xl">
            Discover Your Village Facilities
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl animate-fade-up text-base text-primary-foreground/90 md:text-lg">
            Search any village and explore its facilities, resources and infrastructure through an
            interactive map.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-8 w-full max-w-2xl animate-fade-up">
            <VillageSearch
              size="hero"
              placeholder="Search for a village or ask about a facility..."
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
            <Button size="lg" variant="secondary" asChild>
              <Link to="/ask">Ask about a village</Link>
            </Button>
            <Button size="lg" variant="outline" className="bg-background/80" asChild>
              <Link to="/explore">View map</Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-14">
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
          <StatCard label="Villages mapped" value={villages.length} />
          <StatCard label="Facilities recorded" value={stats.total} />
          <StatCard label="Available" value={stats.available} tone="available" />
          <StatCard label="Need maintenance" value={stats.maintenance} tone="maintenance" />
          <StatCard label="Reported unavailable" value={stats.unavailable} tone="unavailable" />
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-card/60 py-14">
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">What you can look up</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Every category below is searchable for any village you look up.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6">
            {CATEGORY_LIST.map((cat) => (
              <Link
                key={cat}
                to="/facilities"
                search={{ category: cat }}
                className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-background p-4 text-center shadow-soft transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-lift"
              >
                <span className="text-2xl">{CATEGORY_META[cat].icon}</span>
                <span className="text-sm font-medium font-ui">{CATEGORY_META[cat].label}</span>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-14">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">How facility status is shown</h2>
        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {STATUS_LIST.map((status) => (
            <div key={status} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
              <span className={`inline-block h-3 w-3 rounded-full ${STATUS_META[status].dot}`} />
              <h3 className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold">{STATUS_META[status].label}</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">
                {status === "unverified"
                  ? "No verified record yet — this never means the facility does not exist."
                  : "Based on the latest verified record for that facility."}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  tone?: "available" | "maintenance" | "unavailable";
}) {
  const color =
    tone === "available"
      ? "text-status-available"
      : tone === "maintenance"
        ? "text-status-maintenance"
        : tone === "unavailable"
          ? "text-status-unavailable"
          : "text-primary";
  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-up rounded-xl border bg-card p-5 shadow-soft">
      <p className={`font-display text-3xl font-semibold ${color}`}>{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/routes/report.tsx
================================================================================

import { createFileRoute, Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { z } from "zod";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";
import { getAllVillages, getVillageAssets, villageLabel } from "@/lib/villages";
import { toast } from "sonner";

const REPORT_TYPES = [
  { value: "missing_facility", label: "Missing facility" },
  { value: "incorrect_information", label: "Incorrect information" },
  { value: "damaged_facility", label: "Damaged facility" },
  { value: "no_longer_available", label: "Facility no longer available" },
  { value: "incorrect_location", label: "Incorrect location" },
  { value: "other", label: "Other issue" },
] as const;

export const Route = createFileRoute("/report")({
  validateSearch: z.object({ villageId: z.string().optional(), assetId: z.string().optional() }),
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "Report a facility issue — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content: "Tell us about a missing, damaged or incorrectly listed village facility.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Report a facility issue — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content: "Tell us about a missing, damaged or incorrectly listed village facility.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: ReportPage,
});

function ReportPage() {
  const search = Route.useSearch();
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [villageId, setVillageId] = useState(search.villageId ?? "");
  const [assetId, setAssetId] = useState(search.assetId ?? "");
  const [type, setType] = useState<string>("missing_facility");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const { data: villages = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["villages"], queryFn: getAllVillages });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["assets", villageId],
    queryFn: () => getVillageAssets(villageId),
    enabled: Boolean(villageId),
  });
  const { data: myReports = [], refetch } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["my-reports", user?.id],
    enabled: Boolean(user),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("reports")
        .select("id, report_type, description, status, created_at")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data ?? [];
    },
  });

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!user) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const { error } = await supabase.from("reports").insert({
      user_id: user.id,
      village_id: villageId || null,
      asset_id: assetId || null,
      report_type: type as never,
      description,
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(error.message);
      return;
    }
    toast.success("Report submitted. An administrator will review it.");
    setDescription("");
    setAssetId("");
    void refetch();
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-16 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Loading…
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4 py-20 text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Sign in to report an issue</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Reports are linked to your account so administrators can follow up.
          </p>
          <Button className="mt-6" onClick={() => navigate({ to: "/auth" })}>
            Sign in
          </Button>
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Report an issue</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Help keep facility information accurate for everyone.
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 space-y-4 rounded-2xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-soft">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Village</Label>
            <select
              value={villageId}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setVillageId(e.target.value);
                setAssetId("");
              }}
              className="h-11 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
            >
              <option value="">Not listed / not sure</option>
              {villages.map((v) => (
                <option key={v.id} value={v.id}>
                  {villageLabel(v)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {villageId && assets.length ? (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label>Facility (optional)</Label>
              <select
                value={assetId}
                onChange={(e) => setAssetId(e.target.value)}
                className="h-11 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
              >
                <option value="">Not about a specific facility</option>
                {assets.map((a) => (
                  <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>
                    {a.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label>Issue type</Label>
            <select
              value={type}
              onChange={(e) => setType(e.target.value)}
              className="h-11 w-full rounded-md border bg-background px-3 text-sm font-ui"
            >
              {REPORT_TYPES.map((t) => (
                <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>
                  {t.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="description">What should we know?</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="description"
              required
              rows={5}
              value={description}
              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Describe the facility and what is wrong or missing."
            />
          </div>

          <Button type="submit" disabled={busy || !description.trim()}>
            {busy ? "Submitting…" : "Submit report"}
          </Button>
        </form>

        <section className="mt-10">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Your reports</h2>
          {myReports.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3">
              {myReports.map((r) => (
                <li key={r.id} className="rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                      {REPORT_TYPES.find((t) => t.value === r.report_type)?.label ?? r.report_type}
                    </p>
                    <span className="rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs capitalize font-ui">
                      {r.status.replace("_", " ")}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{r.description}</p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
              You haven't submitted any reports yet.{" "}
              <Link to="/explore" className="text-primary underline">
                Explore the map
              </Link>
              .
            </p>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/routes/village.$villageId.tsx
================================================================================

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import FacilityMap, { type MapMarkerPoint } from "@/components/FacilityMap";
import { FacilityCard } from "@/components/FacilityCard";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  getVillage,
  getVillageAssets,
  summarise,
  villageLabel,
  type AssetRow,
} from "@/lib/villages";
import { CATEGORY_META, STATUS_META, type AssetCategory } from "@/lib/categories";
import { searchNearbyPlaces, type NearbyPlace } from "@/lib/maps.functions";
import { useAuth } from "@/hooks/useAuth";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/village/$villageId")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
      { title: "Village facility dashboard — GramMap" },
      {
        name: "description",
        content:
          "Facility availability, condition and map locations recorded for the selected village.",
      },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Village facility dashboard — GramMap" },
      {
        property: "og:description",
        content:
          "Facility availability, condition and map locations recorded for the selected village.",
      },
    ],
  }),
  component: VillagePage,
});

function VillagePage() {
  const { villageId } = Route.useParams();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<AssetCategory | "all">("all");
  const [focus, setFocus] = useState<{ lat: number; lng: number } | null>(null);
  const [nearby, setNearby] = useState<NearbyPlace[] | null>(null);
  const [nearbyError, setNearbyError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [nearbyLoading, setNearbyLoading] = useState(false);

  const { data: village, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["village", villageId],
    queryFn: () => getVillage(villageId),
  });
  const { data: assets = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["assets", villageId],
    queryFn: () => getVillageAssets(villageId),
  });

  const stats = summarise(assets);
  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => (category === "all" ? assets : assets.filter((a) => a.category === category)),
    [assets, category],
  );

  const categoriesPresent = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = new Map<AssetCategory, number>();
    assets.forEach((a) => counts.set(a.category, (counts.get(a.category) ?? 0) + 1));
    return [...counts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  }, [assets]);

  const markers: MapMarkerPoint[] = useMemo(() => {
    const inside = filtered
      .filter((a) => a.latitude != null && a.longitude != null)
      .map((a) => ({
        id: a.id,
        name: a.name,
        subtitle: CATEGORY_META[a.category].label,
        latitude: Number(a.latitude),
        longitude: Number(a.longitude),
        category: a.category,
        status: a.status,
      }));
    const outside = (nearby ?? []).map((p) => ({
      id: p.id,
      name: p.name,
      subtitle: p.address,
      latitude: p.latitude,
      longitude: p.longitude,
      external: true,
    }));
    return [...inside, ...outside];
  }, [filtered, nearby]);

  const center =
    focus ??
    (village?.latitude != null && village?.longitude != null
      ? { lat: Number(village.latitude), lng: Number(village.longitude) }
      : null);

  async function runNearby(keyword: string) {
    if (!village?.latitude || !village.longitude) return;
    setNearbyLoading(true);
    setNearbyError(null);
    try {
      const res = await searchNearbyPlaces({
        data: {
          latitude: Number(village.latitude),
          longitude: Number(village.longitude),
          keyword,
          radiusMeters: 10000,
        },
      });
      setNearby(res.places);
    } catch {
      setNearbyError("Nearby search needs you to be signed in, and it may be briefly unavailable.");
      setNearby([]);
    } finally {
      setNearbyLoading(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-16 text-muted-foreground font-ui">Loading…</div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  if (!village) {
    return (
      <AppShell>
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4 py-20 text-center">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">This village is not in GramMap</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Try locating it on the map instead.
          </p>
          <Button className="mt-6" asChild>
            <Link to="/explore">Open the map</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </AppShell>
    );
  }

  const available = filtered.filter((a) => a.status === "available");
  const maintenance = filtered.filter((a) => a.status === "maintenance");
  const unavailable = filtered.filter((a) => a.status === "unavailable");
  const unverified = filtered.filter((a) => a.status === "unverified");

  return (
    <AppShell>
      <div className="bg-field-gradient">
        <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-10">
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Selected village
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-semibold md:text-4xl">{village.name}</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">{villageLabel(village)}</p>
          {village.description ? (
            <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">{village.description}</p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-5">
            <Stat label="Total facilities" value={stats.total} />
            <Stat label="Available" value={stats.available} className="text-status-available" />
            <Stat
              label="Maintenance"
              value={stats.maintenance}
              className="text-status-maintenance"
            />
            <Stat
              label="Unavailable"
              value={stats.unavailable}
              className="text-status-unavailable"
            />
            <Stat label="Not verified" value={stats.unverified} className="text-muted-foreground" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl px-4 py-8">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <FilterChip active={category === "all"} onClick={() => setCategory("all")}>
            All ({assets.length})
          </FilterChip>
          {categoriesPresent.map(([cat, count]) => (
            <FilterChip key={cat} active={category === cat} onClick={() => setCategory(cat)}>
              {CATEGORY_META[cat].icon} {CATEGORY_META[cat].label} ({count})
            </FilterChip>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <FacilityMap markers={markers} center={center} zoom={focus ? 16 : 14} className="h-[420px]" />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-10 space-y-10">
          <Section title="Available facilities" assets={available} onShowOnMap={setFocusFrom(setFocus)} />
          <Section
            title="Facilities needing maintenance"
            assets={maintenance}
            onShowOnMap={setFocusFrom(setFocus)}
          />
          <Section
            title="Unavailable facilities"
            assets={unavailable}
            onShowOnMap={setFocusFrom(setFocus)}
            note="Reported as not working. Conditions can change — report an update if you know more."
          />
          <Section
            title="Not verified yet"
            assets={unverified}
            onShowOnMap={setFocusFrom(setFocus)}
            note="No verified record yet. This does not mean the facility does not exist."
          />
        </div>

        <section className="mt-12 rounded-2xl border bg-card p-6 shadow-soft">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Nearby / outside village</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
            Can't find what you need inside {village.name}? Search Google Maps around the village.
            Results here are never mixed with recorded village facilities.
          </p>
          {user ? (
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {(["hospital", "school", "bank", "bus stop", "pharmacy"] as const).map((k) => (
                <Button key={k} size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => void runNearby(k)}>
                  {k}
                </Button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-ui">
              <Link to="/auth" className="text-primary underline">
                Sign in
              </Link>{" "}
              to search nearby places.
            </p>
          )}
          {nearbyLoading ? (
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">Searching nearby…</p>
          ) : null}
          {nearbyError ? (
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-destructive font-ui">{nearbyError}</p>
          ) : null}
          {nearby?.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-4 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
              {nearby.map((p) => (
                <li key={p.id} className="rounded-xl border border-sky/40 bg-sky/10 p-3">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{p.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{p.address}</p>
                  <a
                    className="mt-1 inline-block text-xs text-primary underline font-ui"
                    href={`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${p.latitude},${p.longitude}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                  >
                    Get directions
                  </a>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
        </section>

        <div className="mt-8">
          <Button variant="outline" asChild>
            <Link to="/report" search={{ villageId: village.id }}>
              Report an issue in this village
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

function setFocusFrom(setFocus: (c: { lat: number; lng: number }) => void) {
  return (asset: AssetRow) => {
    if (asset.latitude == null || asset.longitude == null) return;
    setFocus({ lat: Number(asset.latitude), lng: Number(asset.longitude) });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 320, behavior: "smooth" });
  };
}

function Stat({ label, value, className }: { label: string; value: number; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="animate-fade-up rounded-xl border bg-card p-4 shadow-soft">
      <p className={`font-display text-2xl font-semibold ${className ?? "text-primary"}`}>{value}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-ui">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function FilterChip({
  active,
  onClick,
  children,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors font-ui ${
        active ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-card hover:bg-accent/30"
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function Section({
  title,
  assets,
  note,
  onShowOnMap,
}: {
  title: string;
  assets: AssetRow[];
  note?: string;
  onShowOnMap: (a: AssetRow) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <section>
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      {note ? <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">{note}</p> : null}
      {assets.length ? (
        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {assets.map((a) => (
            <FacilityCard key={a.id} asset={a} onShowOnMap={onShowOnMap} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed bg-muted/30 p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground font-ui">
          Facility information is currently unavailable.
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export const statusLabels = STATUS_META;

FILE: src/server.ts

import "./lib/error-capture";

import { consumeLastCapturedError } from "./lib/error-capture";
import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";

type ServerEntry = {
  fetch: (request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) => Promise<Response> | Response;
};

let serverEntryPromise: Promise<ServerEntry> | undefined;

async function getServerEntry(): Promise<ServerEntry> {
  if (!serverEntryPromise) {
    serverEntryPromise = import("@tanstack/react-start/server-entry").then(
      (m) => (m.default ?? m) as ServerEntry,
    );
  }
  return serverEntryPromise;
}

// h3 swallows in-handler throws into a normal 500 Response with body
// {"unhandled":true,"message":"HTTPError"} — try/catch alone never fires for those.
async function normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response: Response): Promise<Response> {
  if (response.status < 500) return response;
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) return response;

  const body = await response.clone().text();
  if (!isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body)) return response;

  console.error(consumeLastCapturedError() ?? new Error(`h3 swallowed SSR error: ${body}`));
  return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
    status: 500,
    headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

function isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body: string): boolean {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(body) as { unhandled?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return payload.unhandled === true && payload.message === "HTTPError";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) {
    try {
      const handler = await getServerEntry();
      const response = await handler.fetch(request, env, ctx);
      return await normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
      });
    }
  },
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/start.ts

import { createStart, createCsrfMiddleware, createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";
import { attachSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-attacher";

const errorMiddleware = createMiddleware().server(async ({ next }) => {
  try {
    return await next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error != null && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error(error);
    return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
    });
  }
});

// Start installs this automatically when src/start.ts is absent; defining the
// file opts out, so re-add it explicitly to keep server functions protected
// from cross-site requests.
const csrfMiddleware = createCsrfMiddleware({
  filter: (ctx) => ctx.handlerType === "serverFn",
});

export const startInstance = createStart(() => ({
  functionMiddleware: [attachSupabaseAuth],
  requestMiddleware: [errorMiddleware, csrfMiddleware],
}));

@import "tailwindcss" source(none);
@source "../src";

@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));

/*
 * Design system definition.
 *
 * The @theme inline block maps CSS custom properties to Tailwind utility
 * classes (e.g. --color-primary -> bg-primary, text-primary).
 *
 * The :root and .dark blocks define the actual color values using oklch.
 * All colors MUST use oklch format.
 *
 * To add a new semantic color:
 * 1. Add the variable to :root (light value) and .dark (dark value)
 * 2. Register it in @theme inline as --color-<name>: var(--<name>)
 */

@theme inline {
  --radius-sm: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  --radius-md: calc(var(--radius) - 2px);
  --radius-lg: var(--radius);
  --radius-xl: calc(var(--radius) + 4px);
  --radius-2xl: calc(var(--radius) + 8px);

================================================================================
FILE: src/types/google-maps.d.ts
================================================================================

// Minimal ambient declarations for the Google Maps JS API surface GramMap uses.
// The build environment cannot resolve @types/google.maps, so we declare the
// small subset we need instead of depending on that package.

declare namespace google {
  namespace maps {
    interface LatLngLiteral {
      lat: number;
      lng: number;
    }

    interface MapOptions {
      center?: LatLngLiteral;
      zoom?: number;
      clickableIcons?: boolean;
      mapTypeControl?: boolean;
      streetViewControl?: boolean;
      styles?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
      [key: string]: unknown;
    }

    interface MapsEventListener {
      remove(): void;
    }

    interface MapMouseEvent {
      latLng: { lat(): number; lng(): number } | null;
    }

    class Map {
      constructor(el: HTMLElement, opts?: MapOptions);
      addListener(event: string, handler: (e: MapMouseEvent) => void): MapsEventListener;
      setCenter(center: LatLngLiteral): void;
      setZoom(zoom: number): void;
      fitBounds(bounds: LatLngBounds): void;
    }

    interface MarkerOptions {
      map?: Map | null;
      position?: LatLngLiteral;
      title?: string;
      icon?: {
        path: SymbolPath | string;
        scale?: number;
        fillColor?: string;
        fillOpacity?: number;
        strokeColor?: string;
        strokeWeight?: number;
      };
      [key: string]: unknown;
    }

    class Marker {
      constructor(opts?: MarkerOptions);
      setMap(map: Map | null): void;
      addListener(event: string, handler: () => void): MapsEventListener;
    }

    class InfoWindow {
      constructor(opts?: Record<string, unknown>);
      setContent(content: string | HTMLElement): void;
      open(opts: { map: Map; anchor?: Marker }): void;
      close(): void;
    }

    class LatLngBounds {
      constructor();
      extend(point: LatLngLiteral): void;
    }

    enum SymbolPath {
      CIRCLE,
      BACKWARD_CLOSED_ARROW,
      BACKWARD_OPEN_ARROW,
      FORWARD_CLOSED_ARROW,
      FORWARD_OPEN_ARROW,
    }
  }
}

interface Window {
  google?: typeof google;
}

// @lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config already includes the following — do NOT add them manually
// or the app will break with duplicate plugins:
//   - TanStack devtools (dev-only, first), tanstackStart, viteReact, tailwindcss, tsConfigPaths,
//     nitro (build-only using cloudflare as a default target), VITE_* env injection, @ path alias,
//     React/TanStack dedupe, error logger plugins, and sandbox detection (port/host/strictPort).
// You can pass additional config via defineConfig({ vite: { ... }, etc... }) if needed.
import { defineConfig } from "@lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config";

export default defineConfig({
  tanstackStart: {
    // Redirect TanStack Start's bundled server entry to src/server.ts (our SSR error wrapper).
    // nitro/vite builds from this
    server: { entry: "server" },
  },
});
